# Skills · B · part 41

848 entries, bhavya-reasons-canvas to biblio-check. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **bhavya-reasons-canvas**: Generate REASONS Canvas design contracts for Bhavya Foundation. Use when creating structured design artifacts for substantial features, architectural changes, or cross-module work. Integrates OpenSPDD methodology into ICM workflow. ([thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS](https://github.com/thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/bhavya-reasons-canvas))
- **bhavya-route-audit**: Audit routes, navigation, and links for completeness. Use when adding/modifying routes, navigation, CTAs, or redirects. ([thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS](https://github.com/thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/bhavya-route-audit))
- **bhavya-security**: Verify security practices in Bhavya code. Use when adding authentication, handling user input, creating API endpoints, or implementing sensitive features. ([thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS](https://github.com/thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/bhavya-security))
- **bhavya-spec**: Structured specification development for Bhavya OS. Adapted from gstack /spec. ([thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS](https://github.com/thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/bhavya-spec))
- **bhavya-ui-verification**: Verify UI implementation against design system and accessibility standards. Use after completing visual/interactive work. ([thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS](https://github.com/thebhavyafoundation/Bhavya-OS/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/bhavya-ui-verification))
- **bhe-bootstrap**: Establish a working session against a BloodHound Enterprise deployment — verify credentials, identify the environment, list domains, and confirm the API is responding. Run this at the start of every session before any other BHE skill, or whenever a tool fails because no credentials are configured. ([dreadnode/capabilities](https://github.com/dreadnode/capabilities/tree/HEAD/capabilities/bloodhound-enterprise/skills/bhe-bootstrap) · ★22 · 2 copies)
- **bhela-release**: Performs a full versioned release for the BHELA WordPress project. Triggers on requests like: "make release", "do a release", "release the project", "publish a new version", "create release". ([shauncuier/BhelaHouseBoatWP](https://github.com/shauncuier/BhelaHouseBoatWP/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/bhela-release))
- **bhela-test**: Run the BHELA regression suite (accounting, security, admin UI, OTP, SMS gateway) and report what broke. Use before any release, after touching roles, capabilities, pricing, cost sheets, the statement, salary or an admin screen, and whenever the user asks to run the tests. ([shauncuier/BhelaHouseBoatWP](https://github.com/shauncuier/BhelaHouseBoatWP/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bhela-test))
- **bhengine-template**: Write, debug and ship a .bhe template for the bhex hex editor, in any directory. Use when writing a template that decodes a file format, editing or fixing an existing .bhe file, or debugging a template runtime error ("Exception @ line N"). Covers the bhengine language, its builtins, and the measure-then-read idiom that variable-length and text-oriented formats need. ([borzacchiello/bhex](https://github.com/borzacchiello/bhex/tree/HEAD/skills/bhengine-template) · ★8)
- **bhex-usage**: Drive the bhex hex editor from the shell — inspect, search, hash, diff, disassemble and patch a binary file, interactively or from a script. Use when answering "what is in this file", carving or extracting bytes, applying a binary patch, or automating any of that with `bhex -c` / `bhex -s`. Covers the command grammar, the current-offset model, backtick expressions and the write/commit workflow. ([borzacchiello/bhex](https://github.com/borzacchiello/bhex/tree/HEAD/skills/bhex-usage) · ★8)
- **bhived**: Search bhived's shared lessons before specialized, unfamiliar, risky, or medium/hard work, and write back verified fixes, dead ends, and version changes. Use it before you guess, after 2 failed attempts, when unsure about a version or API, on a confusing error, or when the user corrects you. Needs the bhived MCP server (bhived_query, bhived_write_instruction, bhived_write_mistake, bhived_write_update). ([ArtKeyAi/bhived-mcp](https://github.com/ArtKeyAi/bhived-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/bhived/skills/bhived) · 2 copies)

- **bias**: The Science hand for name the selection effect before the result. You state the hypothesis before the data and report the uncertainty with the estimate. A result without an error bar is a story with a decimal point. Use when the work touches a measurement, when the user says "an experiment", "a measurement", or "a hypothesis", or when they ask "bias", "how do I name the selection effect before the result", or "bias keeps failing". Also use when a measurement must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/bias))
- **bias**: You are a cognitive psychologist specializing in bias identification and mitigation. - **bias-and-independence-review**: Tests anchoring, confirmation, political, sponsor, sunk-cost, optimism, groupthink, selection, and model biases; checks reviewer independence and dissent quality. - **bias-assessment**: Use when loading datasets, training ML models, evaluating model performance, or preparing data for AI systems. Do NOT skip this for "small" or "simple" models. ([obielin/responsible-ai-skills](https://github.com/obielin/responsible-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bias-assessment) · ★2)
- **bias-assessment**: Evaluate AI systems for fairness using demographic parity, equalized odds, and bias detection techniques with mitigation strategies. ([DTMC-marketplace/governance](https://github.com/DTMC-marketplace/governance/tree/HEAD/skills/bias-assessment))
- **bias-assessor**: Use when `evidence-review` has an extraction table and needs lightweight risk-of-bias fields. ([WILLOSCAR/research-units-pipeline-skills](https://github.com/WILLOSCAR/research-units-pipeline-skills/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/bias-assessor) · ★519)
- **bias-assessor**: When quantifying fairness metrics and demographic disparities in AI systems, especially those making decisions about humans (hiring, credit, parole, diagnosis, content moderation). Required before any fairness audit or production deployment decision. ([UitbreidenOS/UitKit](https://github.com/UitbreidenOS/UitKit/tree/HEAD/professional-stacks/ai_ethics_governance_stack/skills/bias-assessor) · ★14)
- **bias-assessor**: Add bias/risk-of-bias assessment fields to an extraction table and populate them consistently. **Trigger**: bias, risk-of-bias, RoB, evidence quality, 偏倚评估, 证据质量. **Use when**: systematic review 已生成 `papers/extraction_table.csv`，需要在 synthesis 前补齐偏倚/质量字段。 **Skip if**: 不是 systematic review，或还没有 `papers/extraction_table.csv`。 **Network**: none. **Guardrail**: 使用简单可复核刻度（low/unclear/high）+ 简短 notes；保持字段一致性。 ([David-Li0406/meta-skill-evloving](https://github.com/David-Li0406/meta-skill-evloving/tree/HEAD/skill-flow/data/skills-refined-36k/skillsmp/bias-assessor) · ★5)
- **bias-audit**: Audit dataset bias across protected attributes — demographic parity, equalized odds, representation gaps, and intersectional bias. Reports actionable gaps with per-group metrics. ([mkurman/zorai](https://github.com/mkurman/zorai/tree/HEAD/skills/bias-audit) · ★327 · 3 copies)
- **bias-audit**: Systematically check a decision or situation for operating psychological biases using Munger's 25 tendencies framework. ([sethmblack/paks-skills](https://github.com/sethmblack/paks-skills/tree/HEAD/bias-audit) · ★10)
- **bias-audit**: Check reasoning for anchoring, confirmation bias, availability bias, sunk-cost fallacy, and premature closure. ([NlightNFotis/skills](https://github.com/NlightNFotis/skills/tree/HEAD/bias-audit) · ★3)
- **bias-audit**: Audits *training data* for representativeness and collection bias relative to the operational environment — demographic coverage, geographic/seasonal/temporal gaps, sample-selection bias, labeler bias. Distinct from `/fairness-audit` (which audits *model outputs* for disparate impact); this skill audits the *input distribution* before training. Use BEFORE training when data comes from a non-random sample, multiple sources, or human labelers. ([gmanch94/claude-code-aidlc-template](https://github.com/gmanch94/claude-code-aidlc-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bias-audit) · ★2)
- **bias-audit**: Detect, prevent, and mitigate systemic, statistical, and cognitive biases using multidisciplinary frameworks. Use when auditing a decision or analysis for bias. ([charly-vibes/incitaciones](https://github.com/charly-vibes/incitaciones/tree/HEAD/skills/bias-audit))
- **bias-audit**: Audit a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system for gender bias and apply fixes. Use when the user asks to check their RAG, search or question-answering system for gender bias, to test whether he/she wording changes the retrieved documents or the answers, or to apply and compare bias mitigations (blind query, PCF, LEACE). Works in the user's own codebase; the statistics are fixed, validated scripts. ([dhthz/Legal-RAG-LLM-bias-thesis](https://github.com/dhthz/Legal-RAG-LLM-bias-thesis/tree/HEAD/toolkit/skills/bias-audit))
- **bias-audit**: Evidence and audience enforcement for audit/playbook/claims-heavy work ([nikhillinit/Updog_restore](https://github.com/nikhillinit/Updog_restore/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bias-audit))
- **bias-auditor** ([UitbreidenOS/UitKit](https://github.com/UitbreidenOS/UitKit/tree/HEAD/professional-stacks/mlai_engineer_stack/skills/bias-auditor) · ★14)
- **bias-auditor**: 当用户说『AI回答有偏见』『输出性别/地域/年龄刻板印象』『怎么检测模型偏见』『内容要过公平审查』，或在发布面向人群的内容(招聘/推荐/客服/评测)前想做公平性自检时使用。把模型输出当『带视角的生产物』：扫人口群体词·刻板表述·单边归因，标出潜在偏见并给去偏改写建议。理论根基：LGD 三律（有籍·有证·有门禁）。触发词：偏见检测、bias audit、公平性、刻板印象、AI歧视、内容审查、公平自检、stereotype。 ([zhaoxinghua09-cell/agent-skills](https://github.com/zhaoxinghua09-cell/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bias-auditor) · ★5)
- **bias-auditor**: Use when evaluating a person or making a decision with high confidence — audits judgment / attribution / metacognition layers and prescribes per-bias remedies. Triggers on: "편향 점검", "내 판단 비뚤어진 것 같아", "audit my reasoning", "bias check", "확신이 과한 것 같아". ([newkayak12/claude-skills](https://github.com/newkayak12/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/cognition/skills/bias-auditor) · ★3)
- **bias-auditor**: Audits theme data and input composition for systematic biases that could distort prioritization. Flags source concentration, recency bias, channel skew, platform demographic bias, viral amplification, single-source amplification, and sample size issues. Grounded in survey methodology, response bias literature, and social media signal analysis. Triggers: "audit bias", "run bias audit", "check for bias" ([mc-barnes/voc-synthesizer](https://github.com/mc-barnes/voc-synthesizer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agents/bias-auditor) · ★1)
- **bias-auditor**: Audit AI outputs for demographic and cognitive bias by testing counterfactual prompts across protected groups (gender, race, age, religion, disability) and detecting stereotypical associations. Use when deploying models in hiring, lending, healthcare, education, or other high-stakes domains, or when a model's outputs seem to skew by demographic.
